TL;DR: 本文提出FeatureFormer，一种在门控图注意力架构中融入节点级FLOPs、参数量和内存代理编码的神经性能预测器，并发布大规模能耗数据集NNEQ。实验表明其在延迟和能耗预测上达到最优性能，且该编码可广泛迁移至现有预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN和Transformer类性能预测器在神经架构搜索中表现良好，但忽略了节点级计算成本，无法准确建模对性能关键的算子，制约了边缘设备上延迟和能耗预测的精度。

Method: 提出FeatureFormer预测器，在门控图注意力架构中加入显式的节点级FLOPs、参数量和内存代理编码；同时构建NNEQ大规模能耗数据集，支持延迟与能耗的统一评估。

Result: FeatureFormer在延迟和能耗预测上均达到最先进水平，在跨域（out-of-domain）设置下同样表现优异；所提编码方案还能以极小开销稳定提升现有预测器性能。

Conclusion: 节点级计算成本编码对神经性能预测至关重要，该方案不仅自身表现最优，且具备良好的通用性和可迁移性，对未来架构搜索与边缘部署优化有重要价值。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed on resource constrained edge devices, accurate prediction of latency and energy is critical for efficient neural architecture search. Existing GNN and transformer based predictors achieve strong results but largely ignore node-level computational cost, limiting their ability to model performance critical operations. To address this, we introduce FeatureFormer, a neural performance predictor that incorporates explicit node-wise encodings of FLOPs, parameter counts, and memory proxies within a gated graph attention architecture. We also present NNEQ, a new large-scale energy consumption dataset that enables unified evaluation of latency and energy prediction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FeatureFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance across both metrics, including challenging out-of-domain settings. Finally, we show that the proposed encoding is broadly applicable and consistently improves existing predictors with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [32] [Actionable CBFI: Integrating Structural Decomposition and Causal Counterfactual Recourse for Tabular Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27821)
*Sejong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A-CBFI框架，一种基于结构因果模型的诊断-处方一体化可解释AI方法，用于表格机器学习中的可行反事实解释，通过识别协同交互瓶颈并释放抑制性结构锁，将98.3%的干预努力集中在已诊断的根因上，相比穷举因果基线减少76.9%的人工干预负担，同时保持因果有效性和全局追索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI的可操作反事实追索方法面临三大挑战：因果无效性、过高的认知负担和预测失败。穷举因果搜索需要修改多个属性，而SHAP等加性归因方法忽略高阶特征协同作用，在XGBoost等复杂非线性模型中导致次优预测动量和发散性干预努力。

Method: 提出A-CBFI（可操作的基于案例的特征重要性）框架，基于结构因果模型（SCMs）构建。该方法隔离协同交互瓶颈并释放抑制性结构锁，将其转化为定向干预；通过数学上分离主动用户干预空间（L_active）与下游效应，集中干预努力于已诊断的根因。

Result: 在金融和医疗领域的实证评估中，A-CBFI将主动人工干预负担减少76.9%，同时保持与穷举因果基线相当的整体追索成本；将98.3%的干预努力集中在诊断出的根因上；在所有因果可行实例中实现完全相对收敛。

Conclusion: 通过优先处理已诊断的因果瓶颈，A-CBFI在保持因果有效性的同时提供定向且可操作的追索方案，弥合了诊断与处方之间的差距，为复杂非线性表格机器学习模型提供了更高效的可操作反事实解释方法。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) increasingly calls for actionable counterfactual recourse, yet current methodologies face challenges related to causal invalidity, excessive cognitive burden, and predictive failure. Exhaustive causal search algorithms often require modifications to multiple attributes, whereas additive attribution-guided methods, such as SHAP, ignore higher-order feature synergies, leading to suboptimal predictive momentum and diffuse intervention effort in complex nonlinear models, such as XGBoost. To bridge this gap, we introduce actionable case-based feature importance (A-CBFI), a diagnosis-prescription integrated framework for tabular machine learning. Grounded in structural causal models (SCMs), A-CBFI isolates synergistic interaction bottlenecks and releases suppressive structural locks, translating them into targeted interventions. By mathematically separating the active user intervention space (L_{\mathrm{active}}) from downstream effects and concentrating over 98.3% of the intervention effort on diagnosed root causes, A-CBFI enables highly targeted interventions. Empirical evaluations across the financial and healthcare domains demonstrate that A-CBFI reduces the active human intervention burden by 76.9% while maintaining comparable global recourse cost to exhaustive causal baselines. By prioritizing the diagnosed causal bottlenecks, A-CBFI provides targeted and actionable recourse while maintaining causal validity and achieving full relative convergence across all causally feasible instances.

</details>


### [33] [FedEHR-Agents: Federated Agentic Optimization for Automated EHR Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27856)
*Jun Bai,Ruilin Wang,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedEHR-Agents，一种以经验为中心的联邦自动化代理优化框架，用于电子健康记录（EHR）建模。每个医院部署自主临床EHR代理，通过历史记忆、任务评估和TextGrad提示优化来提炼本地建模经验，联邦服务器通过证据引导的经验聚合，将跨医院的互补经验蒸馏为全局元提示，供后续本地细化使用。实验表明该方法在多样临床预测任务上优于本地和联邦基线，并验证了临床建模经验作为传统参数中心联邦学习之外的有效协作对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习方法以模型为中心，仅共享预测模型或其更新，忽略了自主临床代理在历史协作中积累的丰富建模经验；同时医院间的直接协作受限于患者级EHR数据的敏感性，需要一种既能保护隐私又能共享更丰富知识的联邦协作机制。

Method: 每个医院部署自主临床EHR代理，执行数据预处理和模型开发，并通过历史记忆、任务特定评估和TextGrad提示细化来提炼本地临床建模经验；联邦服务器进行证据引导的经验聚合，整合跨异构医院中可靠且互补的建模经验，并将聚合经验蒸馏为全局元提示以支持后续本地细化。

Result: 在真实多医院EHR基准上的大量实验表明，FedEHR-Agents在多种临床预测任务中一致优于本地和联邦基线，并且在不同的联邦规模和LLM骨干下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 临床建模经验是超越传统参数中心联邦学习的有效协作对象，该工作指向联邦自主临床智能的未来发展方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models are enabling autonomous clinical agents to perform increasingly complex electronic health record (EHR) modeling workflows. However, agents deployed at individual hospitals remain constrained by institution-specific data and modeling environments, while direct cross-hospital collaboration is restricted by the sensitivity of patient-level EHR data. Although federated learning (FL) provides a natural foundation for privacy-preserving collaboration, existing approaches remain predominantly model-centric, limiting federation to prediction models or their updates while overlooking the richer modeling experience accumulated by autonomous agents. To address this limitation, we propose FedEHR-Agents, an experience-centric federated agentic optimization framework for automated EHR modeling. Each hospital deploys an autonomous clinical EHR agent that performs data preprocessing and model development while refining local clinical modeling experience through historical memory, task-specific evaluation, and TextGrad-based prompt refinement. The federated server performs evidence-guided experience aggregation to integrate reliable and complementary modeling experience across heterogeneous hospitals and distills the aggregated experience into global meta-prompts for subsequent local refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world multi-hospital EHR benchmarks demonstrate that FedEHR-Agents consistently outperforms local and federated baselines across diverse clinical prediction tasks and remains robust across different federation scales and LLM backbones. These results establish clinical modeling experience as a promising collaborative object beyond conventional parameter-centric FL and point toward federated autonomous clinical intelligence.

</details>


### [34] [SOMTab: Set-Order Mamba for Efficient Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27882)
*Hao Wang,Siyu Zhang,Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOMTab，一种基于Set-Order Mamba架构的高效表格上下文学习模型。它将表示构建与查询条件检索分离：行/列表示通过Mamba状态空间混合构建紧凑嵌入，最终预测保留基于注意力的上下文学习。同时引入DCH-TailMix合成先验来多样化合成数据依赖结构。实验表明SOMTab在接近强Transformer基线性能的同时，实现了更快的推理速度和更低的GPU内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格基础模型的性能前沿由注意力主导的架构占据，注意力贯穿整个建模流程。作者质疑在表格上下文学习的每个阶段是否都需要注意力，旨在探索更高效的架构设计，在保持精度的同时降低计算开销。

Method: 提出SOMTab架构，将表格建模分为两个阶段：1) 表示构建阶段：将无序表格token映射为稳定的隐式槽（latent slots），并使用Mamba状态空间混合构建紧凑的行/列表示；2) 预测阶段：保留注意力机制进行查询条件驱动的上下文学习。此外提出DCH-TailMix合成先验，结合度校正图异质性与混合重尾分布来生成多样化的合成依赖结构。

Result: 在多个表格基准测试上，SOMTab在性能上接近强Transformer基础模型，同时推理速度更快、GPU内存占用更低，实现了更优的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 注意力在表格上下文学习的最终预测阶段仍然是必要的，但在表示构建阶段可以用Mamba等状态空间模型替代，从而实现高效与精度的平衡。这为表格基础模型在资源受限场景下的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models based on in-context learning have recently emerged as strong alternatives to task-specific model fitting. However, the current performance frontier remains dominated by attention-heavy architectures, where attention is used throughout the modeling pipeline. This raises a natural question: is attention necessary at every stage of tabular in-context learning? We introduce SOMTab, a Set-Order Mamba architecture for efficient tabular in-context learning. SOMTab separates representation construction from query-conditioned retrieval. For row and column representations, it maps unordered table tokens into stable latent slots and applies Mamba-based state-space mixing to construct compact representations. For final prediction, it retains attention-based in-context learning to preserve query-conditioned retrieval from labeled context examples. We further introduce DCH-TailMix, a synthetic prior that combines degree-corrected graph heterogeneity with mixed heavy-tailed regimes to diversify synthetic dependency structures. Across tabular benchmarks, SOMTab approaches the performance of strong Transformer-based tabular foundation models while achieving faster inference and lower GPU memory usage, yielding a favorable efficiency--accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond Pairwise Graphs in Science: Hypergraph Adaptive Wavelet Operators for Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27883)
*Rajat Sarkar,Venkataramana Runkana,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO是一种基于超图自适应小波变换的神经算子，通过Chebyshev多项式小波滤波器在超图谱小波域中学习PDE解算子，避免显式特征分解，以线性稀疏矩阵成本实现局部谱核，并在2D/3D结构化与非结构化网格基准上取得最优或接近最优精度，同时具备稳定自回归能力和分辨率等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统通常由解算子建模，但学习这些映射在时变系统中尤其困难，需要同化历史状态并在自回归展开中保持稳定。现有神经算子在非结构化网格或点云上表现不佳：网格中心表示会损失精度，图神经算子仅通过成对边建模，无法直接捕捉网格单元、局部邻域或守恒体积之间的组间耦合。

Method: HALO将域提升到超图并在其谱小波域中学习。通过Chebyshev多项式小波滤波器避免显式超图Laplacian特征分解，以线性稀疏矩阵成本获得局部谱核。其可训练的二元小波尺度被正则化趋向紧框架覆盖，使频率响应适应每个PDE，同时鼓励稳定多尺度谱覆盖。

Result: 在2D和3D的结构化与非结构化离散化基准上，HALO在频率法、Transformer、DeepONet、状态空间和图基基线中达到最佳或接近最佳精度，并保持稳定多步rollout。在数十万点的工业气动网格上与最强固定离散化Transformer相当或更好，同时保持分辨率等变性。

Conclusion: HALO通过超图谱小波框架有效解决了非结构化网格上神经算子学习的难题，以线性成本实现高精度和稳定长时程预测，并在工业规模问题上展示了实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Physical systems are often modeled by solution operators that map input fields, parameters, geometries, or past states to steady or future physical states. Learning these maps is difficult, especially for time-dependent systems that must assimilate history and remain stable under autoregressive rollout. Many neural operators work best on regular, structured grids, while realistic simulations often require unstructured meshes or point clouds to resolve complex geometries; in such settings, grid-centric representations can lose accuracy. Graph neural operators handle these domains through message passing or spectral graph filtering, but pairwise edges do not directly capture group-wise couplings among mesh cells, local neighborhoods, or conservation volumes. We introduce the Hypergraph Adaptive waveLet Operator (HALO), which lifts the domain to a hypergraph and learns in its spectral wavelet domain. HALO avoids explicit hypergraph-Laplacian eigendecomposition through Chebyshev polynomial wavelet filters, giving localized spectral kernels at linear sparse-matrix cost. Its trainable dyadic wavelet scales are regularized toward tight-frame coverage, allowing the frequency response to adapt to each PDE while encouraging stable multi-scale spectral coverage. Across 2D and 3D benchmarks on structured and unstructured discretizations, HALO achieves best or near-best accuracy among frequency-, transformer-, DeepONet-, state-space-, and graph-based baselines and sustains stable multi-step rollouts. The same model scales to industrial aerodynamic geometries: on meshes of a few hundred thousand points it is on par with, or better than, the strongest fixed-discretization transformers, while remaining resolution-equivariant.

</details>


### [36] [There and Back Again: Bidirectional Diffusion Bridges for Multimodality Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27885)
*Gabe Guo,Elon Litman,Thanawat Sornwanee,Jose Blanchet,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BIT（双向图像-文本扩散桥），一种基于随机微积分的双向生成框架，直接从文本出发插值到图像，支持图像到文本的反向遍历，在多项视觉-语言和自然科学评估中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态翻译（如文本到图像）方法存在两个局限：（1）生成路径不直接表示源模态，限制采样算法的灵活性；（2）单向生成，无法实现反向转换（如图像到文本）。

Method: BIT通过随机微积分推导，构建从文本直接插值到图像的扩散桥过程，形成SDE形式，支持灵活多样的采样算法，并通过端点条件设计实现图像到文本的双向遍历，形成统一的双向生成框架，损失函数可扩展到高维。

Result: 实验表明BIT与去噪扩散和确定性流基线相比具有竞争力，并在多个视觉-语言和自然科学评估任务上超越这些基线。

Conclusion: BIT通过源感知生成路径和端点条件过程，提供统一的双向多模态生成框架，在保持竞争力的同时展现了更好的灵活性和性能。

Abstract: Multimodality translation (e.g., text-to-image) is a core generative AI task. However, existing approaches (1) follow generative paths that do not directly represent the source modality, limiting the flexibility of some sampling algorithms; and (2) are unidirectional, preventing inversion (e.g., image-to-text). We propose BIT: Bidirectional Image-Text Diffusion Bridges. In contrast to previous approaches, BIT starts directly from text and interpolates into images, providing (1) a source-aware generative path that enables diverse and flexible sampling algorithms; and (2) an endpoint-conditioned process that can be traversed from image to text, providing a unified, bidirectional generative framework. BIT is derived through stochastic calculus, yielding SDE forms amenable to simulation and tractable loss functions that scale to high dimensions. Our experiments show that BIT is competitive with denoising-diffusion and deterministic-flow baselines, and outperforms them on several vision--language and natural-science evaluations.

</details>


### [37] [TACIT-Switch: Cost-Aware Model Escalation for LLM Agents from Censored Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27911)
*Ji'an Lei,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TACIT-SWITCH方法，通过学习永久性的智能体切换策略，在小型语言模型智能体与大型模型智能体之间进行动态路由，利用教师标注的区间删失干预时间（TACIT）来估计大模型成功概率及切换阈值，在保证成本可控的同时提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型智能体成本低但容易陷入持续性失败模式，大型模型智能体更可靠但成本高。这种可靠性-成本权衡促使需要一种路由方法，能够在适当的时机将任务切换给更大规模骨干的智能体，在成本和成功率之间取得最佳平衡。

Method: TACIT-SWITCH从累积轨迹证据中学习永久性切换策略。它将教师的标注表示为累积风险尺度上的区间删失观测，构建混合治愈阈值模型，估计配对大模型rollout成功的概率以及成功条件下的切换阈值。部署时无需教师参与。

Result: 在机制驱动的多步模拟中，TACIT-SWITCH相比任务级、步骤级和固定前缀路由基线，在同等成本下成功率提升7.4-11.1个百分点。在ALFWorld上取得48.5%（4B）和45.5%（9B）的最高保留成功率，在DABench上达到73.1%。消融实验表明任务特征与累积轨迹风险提供互补信息。

Conclusion: TACIT-SWITCH通过学习数据驱动的智能体切换策略，有效解决了小模型智能体不可靠与大模型智能体成本高之间的权衡问题，在多个基准上取得了最优的保留成功率，且部署时无需教师模型参与。

Abstract: Agents with smaller language-model backbones are less expensive but can drift into persistent failure modes, whereas those with larger backbones are generally more reliable but more costly. This reliability-cost trade-off motivates routing methods that decide when to invoke an agent with a larger backbone: before execution, after a fixed trajectory prefix, or locally at individual steps. Our method, TACIT-SWITCH, learns permanent handoff policies from accumulated trajectory evidence and Teacher-Annotated Censored Intervention Times (TACIT). It represents each annotation as an interval-censored observation on a cumulative-risk scale. The resulting mixture-cure threshold model estimates the probability that the paired Strong rollout succeeds and, conditional on success, the handoff threshold; no teacher is required at deployment. In a mechanism-based multi-step simulation, TACIT-SWITCH improves success by 7.4-11.1 percentage points over task-level, step-level, and fixed-prefix routing baselines at comparable cost. Within that controlled simulation, ablations show that task features and cumulative trajectory risk provide complementary information. With operating points selected on development data, TACIT-SWITCH achieves the highest held-out success among learned policies on both ALFWorld (48.5% with 4B Cheap; 45.5% with 9B Cheap) and DABench (73.1%).

</details>

Abstract: This study proposes a novel framework to estimate parameters for reproducing target multicellular patterns using an agent-based model (ABM). Two major challenges in multicellular ABMs are estimating cell-level parameters (agent-specific variables) and quantitatively evaluating the topological characteristics of multicellular arrangements under stochastic cell proliferation and death. To address these challenges, we integrate two approaches: Betti vectors and inverse surrogate modeling. The Betti vectors obtained through topological data analysis can consistently represent features of a wide range of multicellular spatial configurations. The inverse surrogate modeling enables direct inference of the corresponding ABM parameters from the target patterns. We validated the proposed framework using zebrafish pigment pattern formation, a representative model of pattern formation driven by multicellular interactions. The results demonstrate that our framework successfully estimates ABM parameters and outperforms conventional methods such as PointNet++. Notably, the proposed method, which used only 10% of the training data, outperformed PointNet++, which used 100% of the data, across all evaluation metrics.

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) on graphs aims to adapt a graph neural network (GNN) that is well-trained on the training graph to the test graph, which involves potential distribution shifts that may harm model generalization and test-time inference. While recent efforts have investigated TTA on static graphs, there is still a research gap on dynamic graphs learned with dynamic GNN (DGNN) models, where both structural connectivity and node semantics evolve continuously over time. This makes adapting a DGNN model for reliable test-time performance substantially challenging. To fill this gap, in this work, we propose a novel framework of temporal memory-aware Online Test-Time Adaptation on Dynamic Graphs, named DGOTTA, to effectively adapt well-trained DGNNs during test time. Specifically, the proposed DGOTTA contains three modules: (1) temporal-aware augmentation, to extend the diversity of test dynamic graphs for addressing complex temporal and spatial shifts; (2) memory-aware model prediction, to alleviate catastrophic forgetting; (3) consistency-guided online adaptation, to enforce temporal alignment and memory smoothness. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets and four DGNN backbones demonstrate that DGOTTA significantly improves generalization under diverse distribution shifts and multiple model architectures.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: PhyMamba采用两阶段架构：阶段一使用轻量级Mamba编码器处理BMS信号，通过老化参数化模块生成物理信息老化特征；阶段二使用定制化Mamba预测主干进行多周期预测，将物理知识紧密集成以调节模型内部时间更新，使其朝着退化一致的方向演化。该方法无需显式识别内部老化参数。

Result: 在三个公共数据集和多个预测时间跨度上的实验表明，PhyMamba实现了最佳综合性能，与多种基线相比平均误差降低31.8%，同时提供了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: PhyMamba通过物理调制的方式将电化学老化知识注入序列建模，在提升预测精度的同时保持了计算效率，支持实际电池管理系统中的稳健健康预测部署。

Abstract: Battery health prognostics is a core function in battery management systems (BMSs), yet long-horizon health forecasting from BMS signals remains challenging due to operating-condition dependency and sensor noise. In this paper, we propose PhyMamba, a two-stage physics-modulated Mamba framework that integrates electrochemical aging into sequence modelling. PhyMamba does not require explicit identification of internal aging parameters, which often relies on intrusive measurements. In stage-1, a lightweight Mamba encoder first processes BMS signals and produces a latent representation that is transformed via an aging parameterization module, into physics-informed aging features. In stage-2, a customized Mamba forecasting backbone performs multi-cycle prediction, where physics is tightly integrated to regulate the model's internal temporal updates toward degradation-consistent evolution. Experiments on three public datasets under multiple forecast horizons show that PhyMamba achieves the best aggregated performance, with an overall mean error reduction of 31.8% compared with a diverse range of baselines. PhyMamba also offers an optimized accuracy-efficiency trade-off, which supports practical deployment for robust battery health prognostics.

</details>


### [41] [Is Monte Carlo Tree Search Just Every-Visit Monte Carlo Control?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27985)
*Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）与每次访问蒙特卡洛（every-visit MC）控制本质上相同，四阶段搜索过程可简化为策略下的轨迹采样和每次访问MC更新两个基本操作，二者区别主要是术语上的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS与MC控制通常被视为不同方法，分别用搜索语言和强化学习语言描述，作者旨在消除这种术语上的区分，明确二者在轨迹生成和动作价值更新层面的等价性。

Method: 通过概念映射进行论证：将树策略和回滚策略视为单一演化策略中已学习和未学习的部分，扩展对应首次访问与初始化，备份对应普通的每次访问MC更新，从而将MCTS四阶段简化为两个基本操作。

Result: 论证表明MCTS本质上就是每次访问MC控制，只是用搜索的语言和数据结构来表达。四阶段（选择、扩展、模拟、备份）可以归约为策略轨迹采样和每次访问MC价值更新两个操作。

Conclusion: MCTS与每次访问MC控制在核心机制上是等价的，区别主要在术语和表现形式上。该注记以说明性为目的，旨在使这种等价关系更加明确和易于识别。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and every-visit Monte Carlo (MC) control are usually presented as different methods. MCTS is described in the language of search (selection, expansion, simulation, and backup), whereas MC control is described in the language of reinforcement learning (trajectory sampling, return estimation, action-value updating, and policy improvement). This note argues that, at the level of trajectory generation and action-value updating, the distinction is largely terminological. The tree policy and rollout policy can be viewed as the learned and not-yet-learned parts of a single evolving policy; expansion corresponds to first visit and initialization; and backup is the ordinary every-visit Monte Carlo update. Under this interpretation, the four stages of MCTS reduce to two basic operations: trajectory sampling under the current policy and every-visit Monte Carlo updating. In this sense, MCTS is simply every-visit Monte Carlo control expressed in the language and data structure of search. The purpose of this note is expository: to make this equivalence explicit and easier to recognize.

</details>

*Artem Safronov*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向Gemma-3-1B的逐层比特分配方法，在给定生成质量损失预算约束下最大化性能（降低延迟），通过在TensorRT-LLM中使用SA-PTQ的层敏感度分布，对FFN、Attention和lm_head分层确定精度。在RTX 5090上测试13种W8A8变体，最佳方案为FFN 5+5配置加lm_head，实现11.0%延迟下降且质量几乎无损；若容忍一定质量损失（FFN all26 + lm_head），最高可实现19.1%加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的均匀层量化方法（如GPTQ、AWQ）耗时且资源密集，而MixLLM或TorchAO等分配方法缺乏经过验证的加速效果。作者希望提出一种在满足质量退化预算约束下，能够真正实现可证明性能加速的逐层比特分配方案。

Method: 将层比特分配表述为"在退化预算约束下的性能最大化（延迟降低）"问题。利用先前工作SA-PTQ得到的层敏感度分布，在TensorRT-LLM中使用激活直通模式。按分块（5+5、10+10、all26）逐层确定精度，区分FFN、Attention和lm_head对加速的贡献。在RTX 5090上对13种W8A8变体测量时钟速度，并手动实现TensorRT-LLM的SmoothQuant（因导出失败且lm_head不可用）。

Result: 发现FFN和lm_head的量化/反量化时间成本被整数运算优势所补偿，而短上下文场景下Attention的额外量化步骤反而降低执行速度。最佳方案FFN 5+5 + lm_head实现11.0%延迟降低，质量几乎无损（Top-1一致性98.90%，困惑度增加0.85%）。若接受质量损失（FFN all26 + lm_head），可实现最高19.1%加速。

Conclusion: 逐层非均匀量化比均匀量化在加速-质量权衡上表现更优。建议进一步优化方向包括：INT8融合注意力核、KV缓存量化、使用FP8替代INT8，以及类似FFN的部分Attention量化。

Abstract: This paper proposes a layer bit allocation method for Gemma-3-1B, formulating the problem as performance maximization (latency decrease) given a degradation budget constraint (allowable level of generation quality loss). This approach is different from time- and resource-consuming uniform layer quantization methods that are used in the literature (like GPTQ or AWQ) or allocation methods without proven performance-accelerating effect (like MixLLM or TorchAO). The layer sensitivity profile resulting from our prior work SA-PTQ is applied using the activation pass-through mode inside TensorRT-LLM. For each layer precision is determined individually in blocks, according to a grouping introduced in the prior step (5+5, 10+10, all26), differentiating the contribution of FFN, Attention, and lm_head to the overall speedup. The clock speed was measured for 13 W8A8 variants on an RTX 5090. We find that for FFN and lm_head the time cost of quantization/dequantization is compensated for by the use of integer arithmetic, while for short context lengths, the opposite holds true for Attention: an additional step of quantization slows execution down. We propose a manual implementation of SmoothQuant for TensorRT-LLM which was necessary due to export failures, unavailable for lm_head. The best solution found under joint consideration of all three criteria with minimal degradation was FFN 5+5 with lm_head, providing an 11.0% reduction in latency with negligible quality loss (98.90% Top-1 agreement, +0.85% perplexity degradation). With acceptable quality loss for FFN all26 + lm_head, a speedup up to 19.1% was found possible. We suggest further optimizations: fused attention kernels in INT8, KV-cache quantization, using FP8 instead of INT8 and partial Attention quantization analogous to FFN.

</details>


### [43] [Exact Risk Ratios for Weighted Data Selection in Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28007)
*Guangjian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了选择器（selector）在有界样本选择下的加权最小二乘最坏情况比问题，填补了Hanneke等人（COLT 2025）提出的开放区间d<n<2d内的若干空白。作者证明了F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d，F_w(3,4)=5/3，F_w(4,5)=2，并提出了基于调和量Γ_{d,k}的下界猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hanneke等人在COLT 2025中提出了一个开放问题：在有限数据集上，一个选择器最多选取n个带非负权重的样本交给最小范数ERM处理，其最坏情况下损失比F_w(d,n)在区间d<n<2d内的确切值未知。此前已知端点值F_w(d,d)=d+1和F_w(d,2d)=1，本文旨在填补这一开放区间。

Method: 本文采用统一了几何框架：首先建立损失梯度正张成配置的刚性定理（rigidity theorem），然后对R^3和R^4中的小正基进行分类和结构约简，最后通过一个无维度的极值基论证将符号锥几何转换为五点选择。同时利用显式反例排除若干更短的证明路径，并为所有已证情形构造多项式时间选择算法。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对所有d证明F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d，确认了原文中未证明的论断；(2) 证明F_w(3,4)=5/3和F_w(4,5)=2，填补了最小的两个端点公式未覆盖的情形；(3) 对中间预算n=d+k给出下界F_w(d,d+k)≥1+Γ_{d,k}，其中Γ_{d,k}是基于平衡划分的显式调和量，并在特定结构类上证明这是精确的极小极大值；(4) 三个精确值均匹配该下界，并猜想在整个开放区间内等式成立。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一的几何方法（刚性定理+正基分类+极值基论证）在开放区间内取得了多项精确结果，验证了调和量下界猜想的正确性于若干关键情形，并为所有已证情况提供了多项式时间算法。作者推测该下界在整个开放区间内均为精确极小极大值，为后续完全解决这一开放问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, Shlimovich and Yehudayoff (COLT 2025) posed the following open problem. A selector sees a finite dataset $D \subseteq \mathbb{R}^d \times \mathbb{R}$, picks at most $n$ examples together with nonnegative weights, and hands the weighted least squares objective to the minimum-norm ERM. Writing $F_w(d,n)$ for the worst-case ratio between the loss of the returned predictor on all of $D$ and the optimal loss, they proved $F_w(d,n)=\infty$ for $n<d$, $F_w(d,d)=d+1$ and $F_w(d,n)=1$ for $n \ge 2d$, and asked for the value in the open regime $d<n<2d$. We determine this value in several cases. For every $d$ we prove $F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d$, which confirms a claim stated without proof in the original note. We further prove $F_w(3,4)=5/3$ and $F_w(4,5)=2$, the two smallest cells not covered by the endpoint formula. For every intermediate budget $n=d+k$ we prove the lower bound $F_w(d,d+k) \ge 1+Γ_{d,k}$, where $Γ_{d,k}$ is an explicit harmonic quantity over balanced partitions, and we show that this bound is the exact minimax value over the class of datasets whose whitened gradient systems carry an orthogonal circuit-block structure. All three exact values match $1+Γ_{d,k}$, and we conjecture that equality holds throughout the open regime. The upper bound proofs run on a common geometric spine: a rigidity theorem for positive spanning configurations of loss gradients, classifications and structural reductions of small positive bases in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and $\mathbb{R}^4$, and a dimension-free extremal-basis argument that converts sign-cone geometry into five-point selections. We also give explicit counterexamples showing that several shorter routes fail, and constructive polynomial-time selection algorithms for all proved cases.

</details>


### [44] [When Can Conditional Flow Matching Replace Pointwise Negative Log-Likelihood?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28010)
*Yansen Han,Hongxin Sun,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了流匹配对齐方法中将条件流匹配（CFM）损失用作端点负对数似然（NLL）及其新旧差值用作对数似然比何时有效的问题。对于线性高斯路径，作者给出了端点NLL的精确分解，指出CFM仅在残差相互抵消时才是准确的NLL估计器，并发现特定权重w_sc(t)=(1-t)/t可消除内部残差，但该结论无法推广到一般训练或on-policy对齐场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配虽然支持免似然训练，但现有对齐方法越来越多地将CFM损失直接当作端点NLL使用，将新旧损失差当作对数似然比。这些替换缺乏严格的理论依据，作者希望明确这些替代何时成立、何时失效，为将似然型LLM方法迁移到流匹配提供理论基础。

Method: 作者在线性高斯路径假设下对端点NLL进行精确分解，将其拆分为熵项、加权CFM目标、内部速度-分数残差和边界残差。通过分析离策略总体最优解和on-policy对齐场景，研究了不同权重下CFM作为NLL估计器的有效性，并通过跨维度、分布和几何形状的实验进行验证。

Result: 实验表明：在离策略总体最优解处，普通CFM并非逐点NLL估计器，而权重w_sc(t)=(1-t)/t可消除内部残差；但该正面结果不能推广到一般训练或on-policy对齐。on-policy对数似然比即使在端点分布相同或替代优化后仍可能存在偏差。

Conclusion: 该分解为将似然型LLM方法适配到流匹配提供了理论基础，能帮助区分精确替换与受控代理（surrogate），指导实践中何时可以安全地使用CFM损失作为NLL或似然比的替代物。

Abstract: Flow matching enables likelihood-free training, yet alignment methods increasingly reuse conditional flow matching (CFM) losses as endpoint negative log-likelihoods (NLLs) and their old/new differences as log-likelihood ratios. We characterize when these substitutions are valid. For linear Gaussian paths, we exactly decompose endpoint NLL into entropy, a weighted CFM objective, an interior velocity--score residual, and a boundary residual. Thus CFM-only estimates and differences are exact only when the corresponding residuals cancel. At the off-policy population optimum, ordinary CFM is not generally a pointwise NLL estimator, whereas \(w_{\mathrm{sc}}(t)=(1-t)/t\) removes the interior residual; this positive result does not extend generally to training or on-policy alignment. On-policy log-ratios can remain biased even for identical endpoint laws or after surrogate optimization. Experiments across dimensions, distributions, and geometries support these conclusions and the mechanisms that make inexact ratios useful. **More broadly, the decomposition provides a theoretical basis for adapting likelihood-based LLM methods to flow matching, while distinguishing exact substitutions from controlled surrogates.**

</details>

Method: 提出egRUE（Expected Gradients Reconstruction Uncertainty Estimate），将预测解释纳入不确定性计算中，并将不确定性分解为逐特征的贡献，统一了不确定性估计与XAI。

Conclusion: egRUE通过结合预测不确定性与特征级解释，增强了安全关键医疗场景中的决策支持，明确了预测何时可能不可靠以及哪些特征驱动了不确定性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has strong potential to support clinical decision-making, yet its adoption in healthcare remains limited due to a lack of trust. Uncertainty estimation can signal unreliable predictions, and explainable AI (XAI) can clarify how predictions are made but existing methods treat them separately, providing no feature-level insight into why a prediction is uncertain or which tests to prioritize to reduce it. To address this gap, we propose explainable uncertainty estimation, which unifies uncertainty estimation and XAI to both quantify uncertainty and explain feature-level contributions. We introduce the Expected Gradients Reconstruction Uncertainty Estimate (egRUE), which incorporates prediction explanations into its uncertainty computation and decomposes uncertainty into feature-wise contributions. We prove theoretical properties of egRUE and show through experiments that it improves reliability and interpretability compared to existing methods. A user study with medical experts further demonstrates that egRUE's explanations improve calibrated trust over uncertainty scores alone, increasing confidence in correct predictions and reducing confidence in incorrect ones. By combining prediction uncertainty with feature-level explanations, egRUE strengthens decision-making support in safety-critical healthcare settings, clarifying both when predictions may be unreliable and which features drive that uncertainty.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种经典机器学习架构（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）及其量子对应版本（QSVM、QNN、QCNN、QLSTM）在粒子碰撞事件分类与回归任务中的性能。结果表明经典模型在定量精度上略优，但量子模型以极少的可训练参数达到竞争性精度，特别是QCNN仅用4个量子比特和深度3的电路即可复现深层经典CNN的性能，展现出参数效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验高能物理中，粒子碰撞事件的分类与回归是大规模模拟数据处理中的持续计算挑战，需要在速度与精度间取得平衡。作者希望系统评估经典与量子机器学习架构在该任务上的性能差异与权衡。

Method: 使用CERN Open Data门户的模拟质子-质子碰撞事件（电子-正电子和缪子-反缪子终态）作为数据，以横向动量分量作为输入特征、横向动量大小作为回归目标。对比四种经典架构（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）与其量子对应版本（QSVM、QNN、QCNN、QLSTM），并进行基线多项式拟合分析验证问题非平凡性。

Result: 经典架构（尤其CNN和LSTM）在当前硬件和数据集约束下取得略优的定量性能；但量子模型以显著更少的可训练参数达到竞争性精度，QCNN仅用4个量子比特和深度3的电路即可匹配深层经典CNN的性能；基线分析确认了浅层多项式拟合无法解决该回归问题。

Abstract: The classification and regression of particle collision events constitute a persistent computational challenge in experimental high energy physics, where large volumes of simulated data must be processed with both speed and precision. This work carries out a systematic comparison of four classical machine learning architectures, support vector machines (SVM), artificial neural networks (ANN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks against their quantum counterparts: quantum SVM (QSVM), quantum neural networks (QNN), quantum CNN (QCNN), and quantum LSTM (QLSTM). All models are trained on simulated proton-proton collision events with electron-positron and muon-antimuon final states from the CERN Open Data portal, using transverse-momentum components as input features and transverse-momentum magnitude as the regression target. Classical architectures, and in particular the CNN and LSTM, achieve marginally better quantitative performance under current hardware and dataset constraints. Quantum models, however, reach competitive accuracy with substantially fewer trainable parameters: the QCNN reproduces the performance of the deep classical CNN using only four qubits and a circuit of depth three, pointing to a genuine parameter-efficiency advantage on near-term quantum devices. A baseline analysis confirms that the regression problem is non-trivial for shallow polynomial fits, supporting the relevance of the architectural comparison. These results characterize the trade-offs between classical and quantum approaches under realistic, resource-constrained conditions and provide a benchmark for future studies on actual quantum hardware.

</details>


### [47] [Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting for Forecast Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28116)
*Prasen R. Nuthanakaluva,Nava K. Gaddam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义吉布斯系综加权（GGEW）框架，将预测模型视为专家，通过吉布斯风格的指数变换对归一化预测损失进行加权，并结合数值稳定化、多样性感知分数校正和在线超参数自适应（Local-UCB bandit机制），生成Stable Gibbs、Directional Gibbs-NCL和Symmetric Gibbs-NCL等方法族。在M4竞赛数据和多个外部基准上评估，结果显示该框架具有竞争力但并非普适最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测组合是提升预测性能的可靠手段，但简单聚合规则（如均值、中位数、截尾均值等）的相对表现因数据集、预测时域和部署设置而异，缺乏一个统一且自适应的加权框架来应对这些变化。

Method: GGEW将预测模型视为专家，使用吉布斯式指数变换对归一化预测损失进行加权，并通过数值稳定化、多样性感知分数校正（Directional与Symmetric Gibbs-NCL两种变体）以及基于UCB风格的在线Local-UCB bandit机制自适应学习率、多样性强度和变体选择，避免在每个预测步骤评估完整超参数网格。

Result: 在M4官方竞赛提交数据以及Monash交通、电力、太阳能等外部滚动起源部署数据集上评估，结果表明GGEW在多个基准设置下具有竞争力，但其相对性能随数据集、预测时域、部署协议和预测分歧组而变化，并非在所有场景下都优于简单基线。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不在于提出一个普适最优的方法，而是提供了一个统一的概率框架和系统的实证研究，揭示了吉布斯式自适应加权在预测组合中的潜在价值与适用条件，为未来研究何时以及如何有效使用自适应预测组合方法提供了方向。

Abstract: Forecast combination is a reliable way to improve predictive performance when several forecasting models are available. Simple aggregation rules such as the mean, median, trimmed mean, inverse-loss weighting, and exponential weighting are often strong baselines, but their relative performance can vary across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment settings, and levels of disagreement among base forecasters. We develop Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting (GGEW), a probabilistic framework that treats forecasting models as experts and assigns ensemble weights using a Gibbs-style exponential transformation of normalized predictive loss. The framework extends this basic weighting rule through numerical stabilization, diversity-aware score corrections, and online hyperparameter adaptation. GGEW produces a family of related methods, including Stable Gibbs weighting, Directional Gibbs-NCL, and Symmetric Gibbs-NCL. These variants share one core algorithm and differ only in the score used inside the exponential weighting rule. For sequential deployment, we adopt a UCB-style bandit mechanism, called online Local-UCB, to adapt the learning rate, diversity strength, and Gibbs variant without evaluating the full hyperparameter grid at every prediction step. We evaluate GGEW on official M4 competition forecast submissions and external rolling-origin deployment experiments using Monash Traffic Hourly, Electricity Hourly, and Solar Weekly datasets. Results suggest that Gibbs-style adaptive weighting is a useful and competitive tool across several benchmark settings, although its relative performance varies across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment protocols, and forecast disagreement groups. The contribution is not a universal dominance claim, but a framework and empirical study motivating further investigation of when adaptive Gibbs-style forecast combination is useful.

</details>


### [48] [VICT: Verifier-Instrumented Credit Tracing for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28128)
*Pengcheng Li,Zhengyang Zhang,Dongxu Zhang,Sui Huang,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VICT（Verifier Instrumented Credit Tracing）方法，通过利用终端验证器内部的任务结构（可执行或证据支撑的原子及其依赖关系）来追踪信用到具体动作，将信用分配从 rollout 侧推断转移到验证器侧追踪，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体的强化学习中，细粒度信用分配是核心挑战。现有方法通常将验证器仅视为标量奖励，丢弃了其内部任务结构；虽然通过构造辅助轨迹信号或额外比较来估计动作重要性，但仍未利用验证器本身编码的相关检查，导致信用分配不够精细。

Method: VICT方法在训练时暴露验证器中的可执行或证据支撑的原子，并通过依赖有效的证明边将其追溯回具体动作；仅沿这些边重新分配组相对优势，保留原始终端奖励，在证据不完整或模糊时放弃分配，仅改变训练时的优势张量，无需学习评论者、过程标签或推理时验证器访问。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，VICT相比仅结果训练有显著提升，并与最近的细粒度信用方法表现相当。消融实验排除了密集原子奖励、最终提交信用、时间邻近性和稀疏性作为充分解释。

Conclusion: VICT证明了验证器内部结构可以作为一种更可靠、更细粒度的信用信号来源，将信用分配从启发式的rollout侧推断转变为基于验证器语义的确定性追踪，为长程LLM智能体提供了一种高效且无需额外推理成本的训练方法。

Abstract: Fine-grained credit assignment is a central challenge in reinforcement learning for long horizon LLM agents. Standard objectives often train from programmatically verifiable terminal rewards by broadcasting each sparse outcome to every action in a trajectory. Existing methods typically seek finer credit from the rollout side, constructing auxiliary trajectory signals or additional comparisons to estimate action importance. Although useful, these approaches still treat the verifier that judged success as a scalar reward, discarding its internal task structure. Our key insight is that many verifiable tasks already encode the relevant checks inside their terminal verifier. We propose VICT (VerifierInstrumented Credit Tracing), a training-time interface that exposes executable or evidence backed atoms and traces them back to actions through dependency-valid proof edges. VICT redistributes group-relative advantage only along those edges, shifting credit assignment from rollout-side inference to verifierside tracing. It preserves the original terminal reward, abstains when evidence is incomplete or ambiguous, and changes only the training-time advantage tensor, requiring no learned critic, process labels, branch rollouts, or inference-time verifier access. On ALFWorld and WebShop, VICT improves substantially over outcome-only training and achieves strong performance alongside recent fine-grained credit methods; ablations rule out dense atom rewards, final-commit credit, temporal proximity, and sparsity as sufficient explanations.

</details>


### [49] [Learning to Difference: Adaptive Reversible Differencing (AdaRDiff) for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28134)
*Morad Laglil,Younes Hlal,Marouane El Hadari,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRDiff（自适应可逆差分），一种可学习的广义差分方法，通过加权差分消除时间序列中的趋势和季节性结构，并利用可逆重构恢复预测，支持GPU并行加速，在八个基准上取得最先进精度，且作为即插即用模块可提升多种骨干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间序列预测中，趋势和季节性引入的复杂时间结构干扰学习模型的预测能力。经典的差分方法虽然有效但依赖手工选取的阶数和周期，难以融入现代深度架构，因此需要一种可学习的广义差分方法。

Method: AdaRDiff使用可学习权重对过去时间点进行加权差分以简化序列，得到稳定的残差后进行预测，再通过自回归方式可逆重构预测结果。重构过程可表示为闭式卷积表达式，支持GPU并行。同时采用两阶段训练策略，分别进行时间结构发现和重构学习。

Result: 在电力、天气、交通和能源等八个基准数据集上取得最先进预测精度，参数成本极低。作为即插即用模块，在大多数情况下提升了八种不同的骨干模型（从线性模型到Transformer），线性骨干提升最高达25.9%，iTransformer提升达18.3%，GPU加速最高达33.7倍。

Conclusion: AdaRDiff通过可学习差分的单一算子联合捕捉趋势和季节性，在准确性和计算效率上均优于现有方法，且具有高度的可迁移性和通用性，可广泛集成到各类时间序列预测模型中。

Abstract: Reliable long-horizon time series forecasting is an important yet difficult problem. Trends and seasonality introduce complex temporal structure that challenges learning-based forecasting models. Differencing, which subtracts nearby past values to remove such structure, is the classical remedy, but its reliance on hand-picked orders and periods has kept it largely absent from recent deep architectures. We propose \textbf{\underline{Ada}}ptive \textbf{\underline{R}}eversible \textbf{\underline{Diff}}erencing \textbf{(AdaRDiff)}, a generalized differencing approach that uses learnable weights to simplify the series through weighted differencing with previous time instants. This yields stabilized residuals on which forecasting is performed, after which the removed components are restored autoregressively to reconstruct the forecast, capturing trend and seasonality jointly through a single operator. This reconstruction admits a closed-form convolutional expression, which parallelizes on GPU and yields up to $33.7\times$ speedup over the naive recurrence. We furthermore rely on a two-phase training schedule that separates temporal structure discovery from reconstruction learning, as suggested by a theoretical analysis of the gradient when using a linear forecasting model. AdaRDiff attains state-of-the-art forecast accuracy across eight benchmarks spanning electricity, weather, traffic, and energy, at negligible parameter cost. Furthermore, it is designed as a plug-and-play module: integrating AdaRDiff improves eight diverse backbones, from linear models to Transformers, in the large majority of cases, by up to $25.9\%$ with a linear backbone and $18.3\%$ with iTransformer.

</details>


### [50] [Conditional Diffusion Models for Energy-Efficient Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28142)
*Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,André Snoeck,Chyi-Fu Hong,Shijing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件扩散框架，用于生成电动汽车电池电流剖面，通过潜变量条件编码器和一维U-Net去噪骨干网络，在12k行程的真实商业EV遥测数据集上实现了对电流轨迹的逼真生成，Wasserstein距离低于真实样本间的参考距离，验证了生成样本落在测试集经验变异性范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业送货车队电动化使路由优化从基于距离和时间转向基于能源感知的决策。现有序列模型主要提供确定性点估计或有限的（时间或空间）序列预测，无法捕捉规划运营决策所需的合理能耗轨迹范围，这限制了电动车队在实际运营中的不确定性感知规划能力。

Method: 提出条件扩散框架，结合潜变量条件编码器将行程相关条件（如车辆速度、环境温度）映射到共享表示，并引导一维时间U-Net去噪骨干执行反向扩散过程，生成EV电池电流剖面。

Result: 在包含9辆车12k行程的开放商业EV遥测数据集上，模型生成的电流轨迹能够捕捉主导时间包络和尖锐瞬态事件；Wasserstein距离为0.0029，低于真实vs真实参考距离0.0085；潜变量条件注入相比直接条件注入将Wasserstein距离降低89.1%，MAE降低52.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个用于表征真实运行条件下EV能耗的生成建模框架，为大规模运营场景中的不确定性感知车队规划提供了基础。

Abstract: Electrification of commercial delivery fleets is shifting fleet routing from distance- and time-based optimization toward energy-aware decision-making. Existing sequence models primarily provide deterministic point estimates or limited uncertainty summaries, which do not capture the range of plausible energy-consumption trajectories required for operational decision-making. In this work, we introduce a conditional diffusion framework that generates EV battery-current profiles conditioned on route features such as vehicle velocity and ambient temperature. The model combines a latent conditioning encoder with a temporal 1D U-Net denoising backbone that enables trip-related conditions to be mapped into a shared representation and guides the reverse diffusion process. We evaluate the framework on an open-access commercial EV telemetry dataset containing 12k trips from 9 vehicles. The proposed latent-conditioned diffusion model generates realistic cur- rent trajectories that capture both the dominant temporal envelope and sharp transient events. The model achieves a Wasserstein distance of 0.0029 between generated and measured current distributions below the real vs real reference distance of 0.0085 indicating that generated samples lie within the empirical variability of the test set. We further demonstrate that learned latent conditioning substantially improves performance over direct condition injection, reducing the Wasserstein distance by 89.1% and MAE by 52.8%. This work demonstrates a generative modeling framework for characterizing EV energy consumption under real-world operating conditions, providing an essential foundation for uncertainty-aware fleet planning in large-scale operational settings.

</details>


### [51] [The Approximation Rank of Softmax Attention: Sharp Geometric Laws and Robust Interaction Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28150)
*Yuhe Sui,Jianing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了归一化softmax注意力机制中，哪些几何特性控制其秩复杂度。通过最大行ℓ1近似秩分析，文章分别建立了球面与全球体两种支撑几何下的锐利最坏情况定律：球面自注意力的秩复杂度为Θ_{d,ε}(min{n,(1+β)^{(d-1)/2}})，而全球体几何则增加一个径向自由度，得到Θ_{d,ε}(β^{d/2})。对于固定头，行softmax消除了行标量logit方向，可见的查询-键交互维度r产生r/2的逐实例上界，且该指数在极小极大意义上是最优锐利的。近似交互子空间会引入显式的残差输出误差，并产生容差索引的SVD维度。在84头BERT-base校准集上观察到许多头-温度设置下中等程度的有效维度缩减，并与有限构造性秩上证书呈正相关。这些结果将决定最坏情况温度缩放的支撑几何与控制逐头近似复杂度的softmax可见交互几何区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解softmax注意力机制中哪些几何因素控制其秩复杂度，以解释和预测注意力头的近似行为，并为模型压缩与高效实现提供理论指导。

Method: 采用最大行ℓ1近似秩作为度量工具，分别针对球面与全球体两种支撑几何建立最坏情况的严格上下界（Θ表示法）。同时利用逐实例的r/2上界定律分析固定头的情况，通过构造性下界证明其极小极大最优性，并在BERT-base 84头校准集上进行了实证验证。

Result: 球面几何的秩复杂度为Θ_{d,ε}(min{n,(1+β)^{(d-1)/2}})，全球体几何为Θ_{d,ε}(β^{d/2})（在β≥β_0(d,ε)且n≥C_d e^{β/8}条件下）。固定头的交互维度r给出r/2的逐实例上界且该指数极小极大锐利。实证中观察到中等程度的有效维度缩减，并与构造性秩上证书正相关。

Conclusion: 支撑几何决定了最坏情况下的温度缩放规律，而softmax可见的交互几何控制逐头的近似复杂度，两者应被区分对待。该发现为理解注意力机制的结构性近似提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Which geometry controls the rank complexity of normalized softmax attention? We study maximum-row-$\ell_1$ approximation rank, exactly the least unrestricted rank preserving every bounded vector-valued output. Two sharp worst-case laws isolate support geometry: for fixed $d$ and error $\varepsilon$, spherical self-attention has rank $Θ_{d,\varepsilon}(\min\{n,(1+β)^{(d-1)/2}\})$, while full-ball geometry adds one radial degree and, for $β\geβ_0(d,\varepsilon)$ and $n\ge C_d e^{β/8}$, gives $Θ_{d,\varepsilon}(β^{d/2})$. For a fixed head, row-softmax quotients out row-scalar logit directions: the remaining visible query--key interaction dimension $r$ yields an $r/2$ per-instance upper law, and bounded constructions show this exponent is minimax sharp. Approximate interaction subspaces incur an explicit residual output error and yield a tolerance-indexed SVD dimension. On an 84-head BERT-base calibration set, we observe modest effective-dimension reductions across many head--temperature settings, together with positive associations with finite constructive rank upper certificates. Together, these results separate support geometry, which sets worst-case temperature scaling, from softmax-visible interaction geometry, which controls per-head approximation complexity.

</details>


### [52] [HARTS: Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Hybrid-Attention Models over Arbitrary Rollout Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28158)
*Boyuan Meng,Peihua Bao,Hong Liu,Xiaowei Zhu,Chao Wang,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARTS是一个针对混合注意力（hybrid-attention）模型的代理强化学习系统，通过联合规划微批次、数据并行副本分配和微批次槽调度，利用前缀压缩和非重放紧凑token工作，在树状rollout结构上实现前缀共享加速。实验表明在SWE-bench生成的Agentic RL工作负载上获得4.81-4.87倍的加速，且数值差异与基线自重跑相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理强化学习常产生不规则的、带有共享历史的rollout树。传统方法独立训练根到叶的轨迹，会重复计算共享前缀。现有系统主要针对全注意力模型，缺乏兼容激活重计算的密集可微混合注意力执行方案。

Method: HARTS联合规划微批次、DP副本分配和微批次槽调度；采用非重放紧凑token工作实现前缀压缩；为分块线性注意力设计线性时间算法来协调块边界状态恢复与重放；保留逐轨迹训练的分块状态划分，不重复投影和MLP/MoE计算，仅进行有界状态重放保证数值对齐；每轮将全部分支打包为一次调用，通过可微状态交接传播梯度，支持激活重计算并恢复每个token的对数概率。

Result: 在SWE-bench任务生成的Agentic RL工作负载上，HARTS在多种并行配置下实现4.81-4.87倍的forward/backward/gradient加速（含激活重计算）；数值差异与基线自重跑差异相当；在τ³-Bench训练前120步中奖励趋势与基线相似。这是首个在真实混合注意力模型上展示任意rollout树前缀共享加速的系统。

Conclusion: HARTS证明了在真实混合注意力模型上实现arbitrary rollout树前缀共享加速的可行性，同时保持RL objectives接口不变，支持MoE路由下的语义多重性恢复目标token权重和负载统计，是首个在混合注意力模型上实现该能力的系统。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) often produces irregular rollout trees with shared histories. Training root-to-leaf trajectories independently recomputes these shared prefixes. Existing systems primarily target full-attention models and lack dense, differentiable hybrid-attention execution compatible with activation recomputation. We present HARTS (Hybrid-Attention RL over Tree Structures). HARTS jointly plans microbatches, data-parallel (DP) replica assignments, and microbatch-slot schedules using non-replay compact-token work after prefix compression. For chunkwise linear attention, a linear-time algorithm coordinates chunk-boundary state recovery and replay and produces the minimum number of sequential linear-attention calls under our packed execution model. HARTS preserves the chunkwise state partitioning of trajectory-wise training: it does not repeat projections, MLP/MoE computation, or final outputs, and performs only bounded state replay for numerical alignment. Per round, HARTS batches all branches into one packed call, propagates gradients through differentiable state handoffs, supports activation recomputation, and restores per-token log-probabilities. For deterministic, no-token-drop top-$k$ MoE routing, semantic multiplicities restore MoE-objective token weights and load statistics. Existing RL objectives retain their interface. To our knowledge, HARTS is the first system to demonstrate arbitrary-rollout-tree prefix-sharing speedups on a real hybrid-attention model. On an Agentic RL workload generated from SWE-bench tasks, HARTS achieves $4.81$--$4.87\times$ forward/backward/gradient speedup with activation recomputation across multiple parallel configurations. Its numerical differences are comparable to baseline self-rerun variation, and its reward trend is similar to the baseline over the first 120 steps of $τ^3$-Bench training.

</details>


### [53] [Biologically Inspired Mechanisms for Facilitating Grokking in Multilayer Perceptrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28184)
*Florin Leon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了七种生物启发的神经机制（输入门控、结构可塑性、增益调制、阈值调制、稳态、侧抑制和激活去相关）对MLP中"grokking"（从记忆到泛化的延迟转变）现象的影响。通过系统消融实验发现，稳态（homeostasis）对泛化提升最为显著，结构稀疏化为第二重要机制，其余机制效果较小或不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking是从记忆到泛化的延迟转变过程，常伴随内部表征的大规模重组。作者想知道生物启发的机制（大多数ANN中不常使用）能否通过调节隐藏层计算来主动促进这一转变，从而加速泛化的实现。

Method: 在多层感知机（MLP）上引入七种生物机制：输入门控、结构可塑性、增益调制、阈值调制、稳态、侧抑制和激活去相关，并在两个基准任务（稀疏奇偶校验和噪声XOR分类）上进行系统消融实验来评估各机制的贡献。

Result: 各机制对泛化的贡献不均衡：稳态（homeostasis）提供最强且最一致的收益，结构稀疏化是第二重要的机制，其余生物机制的效应较小或在不同实验中表现不一致。两个问题都支持"显式调控神经元利用率和有效连接性可改善泛化内部计算的出现"这一共同原则。

Conclusion: 研究发现验证了生物启发的活动调控和自适应稀疏化对泛化有积极价值，建议在更广泛的场景（包括大语言模型）中进一步探索这些机制，以加速可泛化表征的形成并减少鲁棒泛化所需的优化时间。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization that is often accompanied by substantial reorganization of internal representations. This paper studies whether biologically inspired mechanisms, many of which are not commonly incorporated into artificial neural networks, can actively promote this transition by regulating hidden-layer computation at the levels of neuronal activity, response, and effective connectivity. We augment a multilayer perceptron with input gating, structural plasticity, gain modulation, threshold modulation, homeostasis, lateral inhibition, and activation decorrelation, and evaluate these mechanisms through systematic ablations on two established grokking benchmarks: sparse parity and noisy XOR classification. The results show that the mechanisms contribute unequally to generalization. Homeostasis provides the strongest and most consistent benefit, while structural sparsification emerges as the second major mechanism. The remaining biologically inspired mechanisms have smaller or less consistent effects in the present experiments. For both problems, the results support the common principle that explicit regulation of neuron utilization and effective connectivity can improve the emergence of generalizable internal computation. These findings motivate broader investigation of biologically inspired activity regulation and adaptive sparsification, including in large language models, where they may accelerate the development of generalizable representations and reduce the optimization time required for robust generalization.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Flat Netlist: Hierarchical Graph Representation Learning for Scalable Analysis of Sequential Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28188)
*Jingyi Zhou,Zhengyuan Shi,Jiaying Zhu,Ziyang Zheng,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepSeq3，一种层次化电路表示学习框架，通过将电路抽象为细粒度组合子图与高层次Super-Node Graph（SNG）的双层结构，结合双图神经网络和状态中心预训练方案，在保证正确性的前提下将有界模型检验（BMC）求解时间降低18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路表示学习（CRL）虽然为EDA任务提供了强大的范式，但实际应用中面临两大障碍：工业网表规模庞大导致难以扩展，以及现有方法未能显式建模寄存器级时间动态特性。

Method: 提出DeepSeq3层次化框架：(1) 将电路抽象为两层表示——由触发器（FFs）划分的细粒度组合子图，以及建模寄存器传输结构的高层Super-Node Graph（SNG）；(2) 采用双图神经网络（GNN）架构在两层上学习表示，同时捕获局部布尔逻辑与全局状态转移；(3) 引入状态中心预训练方案，通过预测FF状态间的可达性来赋予模型对时序行为的深层理解。

Result: 在大规模基准测试上，DeepSeq3展现了优越的可扩展性和更丰富的表示能力，在有界模型检验（BMC）中求解时间降低18%，同时保证正确性。

Conclusion: DeepSeq3通过层次化电路建模与时序增强的预训练机制，有效解决了工业级电路表示学习的可扩展性和时序建模难题，为大规模EDA任务提供了更高效的表示学习方案。

Abstract: Circuit Representation Learning (CRL) offers a powerful paradigm to guide and optimize core Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tasks, but its practical adoption is hindered by the immense scale of industrial netlists and a failure to explicitly model register-level temporal dynamics. To overcome these barriers, we introduce DeepSeq3, a novel hierarchical framework that abstracts circuits into a two-level representation: fine-grained combinational subgraphs partitioned by flip-flops (FFs), and a high-level Super-Node Graph (SNG) that models the register-transfer structure. A dual Graph Neural Network (GNN) architecture learns representations at both levels, capturing local Boolean logic and global state transitions. Crucially, we introduce a state-centric pre-training scheme that predicts the reachability between FF states, endowing the model with a deep understanding of temporal behavior. Demonstrated on large-scale benchmarks, DeepSeq3's approach yields superior scalability and richer representations, reducing bounded model checking (BMC) solving time by 18% while guaranteeing correctness.

</details>


### [55] [Performative Privacy: When Differential Privacy Maximizes Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28198)
*Uddalak Mukherjee,Edwige Cyffers,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"表演性隐私"（performative privacy）概念，将隐私保护学习与表演性学习框架结合，研究数据泄露如何减少未来用户参与。通过理论分析和数值实验证明，当泄露与参与之间的反馈足够强时，有限隐私预算的差分隐私机制在长期效用上可以超越非私有估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护学习常被假设能通过保护用户数据来维持信任和参与度，从而提升长期效用，但这一观点此前从未被形式化证明。作者希望将隐私保护与表演性学习框架结合，从理论上严格分析隐私对长期参与和效用的影响。

Method: 作者建立了一个简单模型：智能体反复贡献数据用于均值估计，当数据泄露时可能离开系统。隐私通过差分隐私机制实现，形成估计噪声与未来参与之间的权衡。作者对该动态过程进行了理论分析，并辅以数值实验验证。

Result: 研究表明，当数据泄露与用户参与之间的反馈循环足够强时，有限隐私预算的差分隐私机制在长期效用上优于非私有估计方法。这是首次为差分隐私不仅作为保护机制、而且从长期效用角度也具最优性提供证据。

Conclusion: 差分隐私在特定反馈条件下可从长期效用角度证明其最优性，为隐私保护学习的实用价值提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving learning is often motivated by the idea that protecting users' data can preserve trust and thus participation, improving utility in the long term. However, this claim has not been formalized so far. In parallel, performative learning provides a framework for studying learning systems whose deployment affects the data they later observe. In this work, we bring these two perspectives together and introduce \emph{performative privacy}, where data leakage reduces future participation. We study a simple model where agents repeatedly contribute data for mean estimation but may leave the system when their data is leaked. Privacy is implemented through differentially private mechanisms, creating a trade-off between estimation noise and future participation. We show, through a theoretical study of the dynamics and numerical experiments, that a finite privacy budget can outperform non-private estimation in the long term when the feedback loop between leakage and participation is sufficiently strong. This provides first evidence that differential privacy can be optimal not only as a protection mechanism, but also from the perspective of long-term utility.

</details>


### [56] [Generalized Context in Cross Attention for Transfer Learning of Disjoint Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28209)
*Kazi F. Akhter,Ibna Kowsar,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CATTLE的跨域注意力迁移学习方法，通过利用transformer投影权重（key、value、query）实现广义上下文学习，无需源域和目标域共享特征即可进行知识迁移，在十个不相关数据集对上表现优于九种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的迁移学习面临特征类型、结构和语义异质性的挑战，现有方法都假设跨域表共享特征，这在实践中不切实际，因此需要一种不依赖共享特征的知识迁移方法。

Method: 提出广义上下文学习（generalized context learning），利用transformer投影权重（key、value、query）而非传统transformer激活来捕获规则级泛化。源域的key投影权重与目标域的query权重交互，实现数据无关的跨域注意力迁移。

Result: 在十对不重叠的源-目标数据集上，CATTLE在平均排名（2.9）和平均AUROC提升（3.7%）上优于九种基线方法，包括机器学习、深度学习和使用大规模预训练模型的迁移学习方法。

Conclusion: CATTLE证明仅从单一源数据集即可学习广义上下文，实现无共享特征条件下的有效表格数据迁移学习，并且优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Unlike images and text, applying transfer learning to tabular data is challenging due to heterogeneity in feature types, structures, and semantics across disparate domains. Existing methods assume shared features across data tables to enable knowledge transfer between domains, which is unrealistic in practice. \mds{This paper introduces generalized context learning to remove the requirement of shared features across domains. The generalized context captured by transformer projection weights for $key$, $value$, and $query$ provides rule-based generalization rather than the domain-specific context conventionally learned from transformer activations. Projection weights for $key$ from the source domain interact with the weight for $query$ in the target domain to achieve Cross-domain Attention Transfer Learning (CATTLE) in a data-agnostic manner. Our experiments on ten pairs of disjoint source-target data sets show that CATTLE can learn generalized context from a single source data set and is rank-wise and statistically superior to nine state-of-the-art baselines, including machine learning, deep learning, and transfer learning methods using large-scale pre-trained models. CATTLE achieves the best average rank (2.9) and delivers a 3.7% average AUROC gain over the baseline methods.} The CATTLE source code is available at https://tinyurl.com/pr5s8ywn.

</details>


### [57] [D-TAIA: Domain-Aware LLM Adaptation for Multi-Task Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28236)
*Sjoerd van Straten,Christine Jacob,Marwan Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了D-TAIA框架，通过参数高效的FM微调实现联合的下一个活动和剩余时间预测任务，在数据稀缺、高熵和分布漂移条件下展现出SOTA或竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPM方法在数据稀缺、高流程熵和分布漂移三种条件下性能退化；已有的FM-based方法要么缺乏处理分布漂移的机制，要么依赖与连续时间预测任务结构不对齐的直接回归头。

Method: D-TAIA框架采用三项核心技术：1）领域感知三元组损失（DATL）预训练；2）基于FAISS的最近邻检索用于剩余时间预测；3）TAIA推理策略在微调过程中保持预训练的序列推理能力。整个方法基于参数高效的FM骨干微调，仅使用1000万参数的骨干网络。

Result: 在四个真实事件日志上的评估中，D-TAIA相比微调LLM和循环神经网络基线，始终展示出SOTA或竞争力的性能。消融研究证实NLP和计算机视觉的技术可以有效地迁移到PPM，但各组件贡献因数据集熵而异。

Conclusion: D-TAIA验证了将NLP和CV的先进技术迁移到PPM的可行性，证明了即使是小规模（10M参数）的FM骨干也能实现优越的PPM预测性能，为资源受限场景下的PPM提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) enables organizations to forecast future process behavior, such as the next activity and remaining time of ongoing cases. In practice, three conditions cause existing methods to degrade, namely data scarcity, high process entropy and distributional shift. While Foundation Models (FMs), especially Large Language Models (LLMs), offer a new paradigm through broad sequential reasoning, adapting them to multi-task PPM under these conditions remains an open challenge. Existing FM-based approaches either lack mechanisms for handling distributional shift or rely on direct regression heads that can be structurally misaligned with continuous time prediction tasks. This paper introduces D-TAIA (Domain-aware Training and Attention-based Inference Architecture), a framework for a joint next activity and remaining time prediction task via parameter-efficient fine-tuning of an FM backbone. Our approach combines domain-aware triplet loss (DATL) pre-training with FAISS-based nearest neighbor retrieval for remaining time prediction, and adopts the TAIA inference strategy to preserve pre-trained sequential reasoning during fine-tuning. Evaluated across four real-world event logs, D-TAIA consistently shows SOTA or competitive performance compared to a fine-tuned LLM and a recurrent neural network baseline. Ablation studies confirm that techniques from NLP and computer vision can be transferred effectively to PPM with only a 10M-parameter backbone, though component contributions vary by dataset entropy.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Online Continual Foundation Model Fine-Tuning for Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28237)
*Sjoerd van Straten,Marwan Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个面向预测性流程监控（PPM）的在线持续微调基础模型（FM）框架，通过损失平台漂移检测自主识别任务边界，并维护包含预训练与任务特定方向的统一知识子空间，在九个事件流上超越多个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPM持续学习方法从头训练紧凑的任务特定网络，存在冷启动问题；基础模型（FM）可缓解此问题，但其在流程挖掘领域的持续微调尚未被探索。

Method: COMPASS采用损失平台漂移检测方法自动识别事件流中的任务边界，并维护一个包含预训练方向和任务特定方向的统一知识子空间，实现在线持续微调基础模型。

Result: 在九个涵盖合成与真实概念漂移场景的事件流上，COMPASS在任务无关和任务感知设置下均超越三个SOTA非FM竞争方法和两个更新策略基线，尤其在循环漂移和复杂长运行案例上增益显著，计算开销可接受。

Conclusion: COMPASS验证了基础模型在流程挖掘领域在线持续微调的可行性，有效解决了PPM中的冷启动问题，为概念漂移环境下的预测性流程监控提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) models are increasingly deployed in dynamic environments where concept drift causes the underlying process distribution to shift over time. While recent work has moved toward online continual learning, existing methods train compact, task-specific networks entirely from scratch, leaving a persistent cold-start problem. Foundation Models (FMs) offer a compelling solution to this problem, but their continual fine-tuning in the process mining domain remains unexplored. We propose COMPASS (Continual Online foundation Model-based PPM with Adaptive SubSpaces), the first framework for online continual fine-tuning of FMs for PPM. COMPASS adapts loss-plateau drift detection to autonomously identify task boundaries in event streams and maintains a unified knowledge subspace including both pre-trained and task-specific directions. We evaluate our approach on nine event streams covering synthetic and real-world concept drift scenarios, across task-free and task-aware settings with multiple backbones and with consistent hyperparameter tuning across all methods. Our approach outperforms three SOTA non-FM competitors and two update strategy baselines, with particularly strong gains on streams exhibiting recurrent drift and complex, long-running cases, while incurring acceptable computational overhead compared to the non-FM competitors.

</details>


### [59] [Spectral Features Dominate BCG Respiratory-Event Detection: A Large-Scale Patient-Independent Comparison of Feature Groups in Sleep Apnea Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28242)
*Israel Campero Jurado,Zoe Bousraou,Lara Benning,Sara Padilla Neira,Alexander Breuss,Robert Riener,Esther Irene Schwarz,Elisabeth Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于电容压力垫的BCG信号进行呼吸事件检测，比较了十组信号特征在155名患者中的判别能力，发现频域特征（尤其是0.1-0.4 Hz呼吸带功率，占30.3%）和时域特征合计贡献67%的判别信息，随机森林和直方图梯度提升在患者独立验证下分别达到AUC-ROC 0.967和0.969。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCG传感是长期睡眠呼吸暂停监测的有前景模态，但尚未明确哪些信号特征对呼吸事件检测最具判别力，缺乏系统性的患者独立特征比较研究。

Method: 使用512传感器电容压力垫与呼吸多导图同步记录155名住院患者数据，从六个空间信号通道提取191维特征向量（涵盖统计、时域、频域、小波、帧能量和非线性复杂度），采用严格的留一患者交叉验证进行二分类，使用随机森林和直方图梯度提升分类器，并进行特征重要性分析。

Result: 随机森林和直方图梯度提升分别达到AUC-ROC 0.967和0.969，AUC-PR 0.977和0.979。频域特征主导判别（呼吸带功率占30.3%，FFT谱形特征占15.1%），时域特征补充21.5%，小波和非线性特征仅贡献10.4%组合。频域与频域和时域特征合计占67%判别信息。

Conclusion: 频域和时域特征的紧凑可解释子集即可在患者独立验证下达到临床相关性能，为未来BCG系统的特征选择提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Unobtrusive ballistocardiographic (BCG) sensing is a promising modality for long-term sleep-apnea monitoring, yet it remains unclear which signal features are most discriminative for respiratory-event detection. We present a literature-guided, patient-independent comparison of ten BCG feature groups using a 512-sensor capacitive pressure mat recorded simultaneously with respiratory polygraphy in 155 patients (52 female, 103 male) undergoing in-hospital evaluation for obstructive sleep apnea. Features were extracted from six spatially distinct signal channels, yielding a 191-dimensional feature vector spanning general statistical, time-domain, frequency-domain, wavelet, frame-energy, and nonlinear complexity descriptors. Under strict leave-one-patient-out cross-validation for binary classification of respiratory-event windows versus event-free reference windows, Random Forest and Histogram Gradient Boosting achieved AUC-ROC of 0.967 and 0.969 and AUC-PR of 0.977 and 0.979, respectively. Feature-importance analysis revealed that frequency-domain features dominate discrimination: breathing-band power in the 0.1-0.4 Hz range accounted for 30.3% of total discriminative information across all spatial channels, and Fast Fourier Transform spectral-shape descriptors of the adaptively preprocessed channel contributed a further 15.1%. AUC and curve-length features provided the main complementary time-domain evidence (21.5%), whereas wavelet-derived and nonlinear features contributed smaller secondary effects (10.4% combined across 59 features). Frequency-domain and time-domain features together accounted for 67% of total discriminative information, demonstrating that a compact, interpretable subset of the full feature library achieves clinically relevant performance under patient-independent validation and providing an empirical basis for feature selection in future BCG systems.

</details>


### [60] [SinkSLOT: Sinkhorn via Sparse Lifted Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28262)
*Ian Hsieh,Soumya Snigdha Kundu,Tom Vercauteren,Reuben Dorent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SinkSLOT算法，通过使用期望切片提升传输计划作为非独立先验耦合来稀疏化Gibbs核，解决熵最优传输中Sinkhorn-Knopp算法的两个主要局限：O(N²)计算复杂度和独立耦合作为参考测度的问题。算法每次迭代成本为O(LN)，且无需去偏即可作为散度使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Sinkhorn-Knopp算法在处理大规模离散测度（N≥10⁴）时存在两个关键缺陷：一是每次迭代需要O(N²)的计算量，难以应用于大规模数据集；二是使用独立耦合作为正则化的参考测度，在中等正则化强度下会将质量分配给高成本的传输边，导致次优的传输结果。

Method: 提出SinkSLOT算法，引入期望切片提升传输计划（expected sliced lifted transport plan）作为非独立的先验耦合，以此对Gibbs核进行稀疏化处理。该方法通过L个切片将原本稠密的Sinkhorn迭代转化为稀疏计算，每次迭代复杂度降至O(LN)。

Result: 理论证明表明：SinkSLOT算法收敛；在L个切片下每次稀疏Sinkhorn迭代的成本为O(LN)；所得目标函数是一个无需去偏的散度。在合成基准实验上，SinkSLOT相比最先进的稠密和稀疏EOT方法实现了显著的加速，并在梯度流实验中展示了该散度的适用性。

Conclusion: SinkSLOT通过创新的稀疏化策略和先验耦合设计，同时解决了Sinkhorn算法在大规模应用中的计算瓶颈和参考测度选择问题，提供了一个高效且理论上完备的熵最优传输替代方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: Entropic optimal transport (EOT) has been shown to offer a computationally tractable approximation to exact optimal transport. However, the standard Sinkhorn-Knopp algorithm has two main limitations. First, given discrete measures with $N$ points, each iteration requires $O(N^2)$ operations, which restricts its use on large-scale datasets (e.g. $N\geq10^4$). Second, it uses the independent coupling as a reference measure for regularisation. This assigns mass to high-cost transport edges at moderate regularisation strengths. We propose SinkSLOT, which addresses both limitations by putting forth the expected sliced lifted transport plan as a natural way to sparsify the Gibbs kernel with a non-independent prior coupling. We prove that: 1) SinkSLOT converges; 2) with $L$ slices, each resulting sparse Sinkhorn iteration costs $O(LN)$; and 3) the resulting objective is a divergence requiring no debiasing. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks show that SinkSLOT delivers substantial speedups over state-of-the-art dense and sparse EOT methods. We also demonstrate the applicability of the proposed divergence in a gradient flow experiment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/cai4cai/SinkSLOT.

</details>


### [61] [Residual-Guided Randomized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28267)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于残差引导的贪心构建方法（Residual-Guided Progressive Construction），用于改进随机神经网络的隐藏层构建过程。通过在每个阶段生成候选单元、用闭式残差减少准则评分、选择最优k个单元并重新拟合输出权重，实现了训练目标的单调递减。在71个UCI基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确率、稳定性和整体排名上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机神经网络（如ELM和随机向量功能链接网络）通过固定输入到隐藏层的随机参数并闭式求解输出权重来实现快速训练，但其性能高度依赖于单次随机的隐藏单元抽取。这种单次且任务无关的特征构建往往导致冗余表示和模型容量利用不足，因此需要一种更有效的隐藏层构建策略。

Method: 提出一种通用的残差引导贪心构建程序：(i) 生成一批随机候选单元；(ii) 通过闭式残差减少准则（ridge正则化目标中每个候选单元引起的精确目标下降量）对候选进行评分；(iii) 选择得分最高的k个单元；(iv) 使用包含直接输入链接的标准设计矩阵闭式重新拟合输出权重。该方法具有模型无关性——仅候选生成部分架构特定，评分-选择-再拟合循环在不同模型间共享。

Result: 在71个UCI基准数据集（涵盖二分类和多分类任务）上的大量实验表明，残差引导模型在准确率、稳定性和整体排名性能上始终优于基线随机神经网络模型，且训练过程保证目标函数单调递减。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过在训练过程中利用闭式残差下降准则逐步构建隐藏层，可以显著提升随机神经网络的性能，同时保持其快速、解析可训练的优越特性，是一种简单且广泛适用的改进方案。

Abstract: Randomized neural networks enable fast and analytically tractable training by fixing the input to hidden layer parameters at random and learning the output weights in closed form; however, their performance critically depends on a single uninformed draw of hidden units. This one shot and task uninformed feature construction often leads to redundant representations and suboptimal utilization of model capacity. To address this limitation, we propose a simple and broadly applicable residual guided procedure that greedily constructs the hidden layer using a closed form residual decrease criterion. At each stage, we (i) generate a pool of random candidate units, (ii) score each candidate by the exact reduction it induces in the ridge regularized objective, (iii) select the top k units, and (iv) refit the readout in closed form using the standard design with direct input links. This procedure yields a progressive training process with a guaranteed monotonic decrease of the training objective. The method is model agnostic: only the candidate generation is architecture specific, while the scoring selection refitting loop is shared across models. Extensive experiments on 71 benchmark datasets from the UCI repository, covering both binary and multiclass classification tasks, demonstrate that the proposed residual-guided models consistently outperform their baseline counterparts in terms of accuracy, stability, and overall ranking performance.

</details>


### [62] [Learning to Transfer Across Modes: Towards Unified Urban Mobility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28273)
*Yixuan Zhao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TransMod框架，通过构建共享的区域级空间表示，将不同空间粒度、不同空间结构的多种出行方式对齐到统一空间，实现跨异构出行方式的城市出行需求预测知识迁移，在目标域数据稀缺时仍能保持稳健预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统中多种出行方式共存且相互依赖，但现有预测方法大多针对单一出行方式开发，且隐式假设源系统与目标系统具有兼容的空间结构，难以适用于多模态场景；同时新兴出行方式历史数据有限，使得跨模态联合需求预测极具挑战。

Method: TransMod构建统一的区域级空间表示，将不同空间粒度的移动系统对齐到公共空间，减少结构不匹配和分布偏移；在此基础上学习数据丰富源模式中的可迁移时空模式，并适配到数据稀缺的目标模式中。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，TransMod一致优于现有方法，并在目标数据有限的情况下提供稳健的预测性能。

Conclusion: TransMod有效解决了跨异构出行方式的需求预测问题，通过统一空间表示和可迁移时空模式学习，缓解了对目标域大量历史数据的依赖，为多模态城市交通需求预测提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban transportation systems consist of multiple mobility modes that coexist within the same city and exhibit complex interdependencies, leading to correlated demand dynamics across modes. However, forecasting demand jointly across different modes remains challenging due to substantial heterogeneity in space and the limited availability of historical data for emerging modes. Existing forecasting methods are largely developed for individual mobility modes and implicitly assume compatible spatial structures between source and target systems, which severely restricts their applicability in multi-modal settings. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{TransMod}, a unified framework for urban mobility demand forecasting that enables effective knowledge transfer across heterogeneous mobility modes. TransMod constructs a shared zone-level spatial representation that aligns mobility systems with different spatial granularities into a common space, thereby reducing structural mismatch and distributional shift. Built on this unified representation, TransMod further learns transferable spatio-temporal patterns from data-rich source modes and adapts them to data-scarce target modes, alleviating the dependence on extensive target-domain histories. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TransMod consistently outperforms existing approaches and provides robust forecasting performance under limited target data.

</details>


### [63] [An algebraic proof of Colombo's difference-power determinant conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28274)
*Kun Li,Li Tie,Peng Wang,Zihan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了Colombo于1928年提出的关于差幂矩阵非奇异性的猜想。对于偶数n和具有两两不同坐标的n维实向量λ，证明了所有d≥n-1的差幂矩阵A_d(λ)的行列式均不为零，从而完成了该猜想中剩余的超临界奇数指数情形。由此得到秩公式rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}对所有d∈N成立。证明方法是将假设的核向量转化为实二元形式，利用其射影实线性因子数（带重数）超过其实际Waring长度这一矛盾来完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Colombo在1928年证明了差幂矩阵在特定指标下的非奇异性和秩的性质，并猜想对所有d≥n-1行列式都不为零。偶数指数的情形已被先前关于距离幂矩阵的结果所覆盖，剩余待解决的是超临界奇数指数d≥n+1的情形。完成该猜想对所有指数的验证是该工作的核心动机。

Method: 作者将问题归约为实二元形式理论。假设存在非零核向量，通过构造将其转化为一个实二元形式，该形式具有超过其实际Waring长度所允许的射影实线性因子数（带重数计数）。这一矛盾论证从根本上排除了核向量的存在性，从而证明矩阵非奇异。

Result: 证明了所有超临界奇数指数d≥n+1下差幂矩阵A_d(λ)的非奇异性，完整解决了Colombo猜想。进而推导出对所有正整数d，秩公式rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}成立。

Conclusion: Colombo猜想被完全证明，差幂矩阵在所有指标下的秩结构得到完整刻画。证明方法展示了实二元形式理论与矩阵论之间的深刻联系，为后续相关研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ be even, let $λ=(λ_1,\ldots,λ_n)\in\mathbb{R}^n$ have pairwise distinct coordinates, and define the difference-power matrix \[ A_d(λ) := \bigl[(λ_r-λ_s)^d\bigr]_{r,s=1}^n, \qquad d\in\mathbb{N}. \] In 1928, Colombo proved that $\det A_{n-1}(λ)\ne0$---and hence $\det A_{n-1}(λ)>0$---and that $\operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=d+1$ for $0\le d<n-1$. He conjectured that \[ \det A_d(λ)\ne0 \qquad\text{for every } d\ge n-1. \] For even $d$, the conjectured nonsingularity follows from previously published results on distance-power matrices. The remaining open cases were therefore the supercritical odd exponents $d\ge n+1$. We prove nonsingularity for all these odd exponents, thereby completing Colombo's conjecture. Consequently, \[ \operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=\min\{n,d+1\} \qquad(d\in\mathbb{N}). \] Our proof converts a hypothetical kernel vector into a real binary form having more projective real linear factors, counted with multiplicity, than its real Waring length permits.

</details>


### [64] [Parser States Already Know: Structure-Conditioned KV Persistence for Structured Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28276)
*Linze Wu,Xinrui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PASK（解析器感知的结构化KV持久化）方法，利用LLM生成结构化输出时的解析器状态信号，为不同层组制定KV压缩保留策略，在极低KV预算下显著提升结构化生成任务的准确率和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化生成（JSON、SQL、函数调用）中一个错误字段就可能导致下游动作失败，而现有的KV压缩方法忽略了生成token在解析器状态中体现的任务关键结构信号（如必填字段、参数、结构边界），导致结构关键信息在压缩中丢失。

Method: PASK将解析器推导出的结构信息转化为层组特定的KV保留决策。具体包括：1）利用任务错误敏感性设置最小保护下限；2）利用注意力输出失真分配剩余KV容量；3）通过离线校准阶段将信号编译为保留策略，在线仅需轻量的结构条件查询。

Result: 在总KV预算为0.33时，PASK在Qwen3-4B的八个BFCL非直播和直播子类别上比最强压缩基线平均提升17.39个百分点。端到端服务中吞吐量最高提升2.2倍，TPOT降低3.3倍，峰值GPU内存仅为Full KV的0.53倍。

Conclusion: PASK证明了将解析器结构化信号融入KV压缩决策能有效解决模型侧KV敏感性与任务级结构化风险之间的不匹配问题，在极低KV预算下同时提升结构化生成质量与推理效率，为LLM代理场景下的KV压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: Structured generation underpins large language model (LLM) agents that produce JSON, SQL, and function calls, where a single wrong field can cause the downstream action to fail. Constrained decoding already tracks parser transitions to enforce formal validity, and these transitions expose how generated tokens participate in schema-critical decisions such as required fields, arguments, and structural boundaries under the active grammar. Existing KV compression largely leaves this task-relevant structural signal unused. We introduce PASK (Parser-Aware Structural KV Persistence), which turns parser-derived structure into layer-group-specific KV persistence decisions. PASK addresses the mismatch between model-side KV sensitivity and task-level structured risk by using task-error sensitivity to set minimum protection floors and attention-output distortion to allocate residual KV capacity. An offline calibration stage compiles these signals into a persistence policy, leaving only lightweight structure-conditioned lookup online. At a targe total KV budget of 0.33, PASK outperforms the strongest compressed baseline by 17.39 percentage points on average across eight BFCL non-live and Live subcategories on Qwen3-4B. In end-to-end serving, PASK achieves up to 2.2x higher throughput and 3.3x lower TPOT, while using 0.53x the peak GPU memory of Full KV.

</details>


### [65] [VISTA: Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28306)
*Zewen Ding,Zezhong Wu,Zhou Tao,Shida Wang,Shizhuo Hou,YongXiang Hua,Haoyu Cao,Linli Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VISTA是一种改进的教师-学生自适应蒸馏方法，通过利用结果验证的rollout将教师分布向学生分布对齐，在不增加额外采样或奖励目标的前提下，在多个数学推理基准上显著提升了OPSD的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD将教师分布视为固定目标，仅更新学生，但特权条件并不保证教师总是为问题推理提供最合适的监督信号。当教师分布与学生有效推理不一致时，这种单向监督会误导学生。

Method: VISTA保留标准OPSD的学生更新，同时使用结果验证的rollout将教师分布向学生分布自适应调整。在每个验证rollout内，仅对教师-学生KL散度最大的前k个位置进行约束性调整，且复用OPSD的rollout和损失函数，不引入额外采样或奖励目标。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25基准上，使用Qwen3模型（1.7B、4B、8B）进行测试，VISTA在所有规模上均取得最高Avg@12，分别比OPSD提升0.6、0.7和2.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果验证rollout提供了有价值的学生监督信号，学生到教师的自适应方向是提升OPSD效果的可行且有前景的路径。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a problem-only student on its own rollouts using dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher that also sees a reference solution. However, standard OPSD treats the teacher distribution as a fixed target along the student's rollout and updates only the student %, although -- even though privileged conditioning does not guarantee that the teacher always provides the most appropriate target for problem-only reasoning. This one-way supervision can therefore misdirect the student when the teacher distribution is misaligned with valid student reasoning. We therefore introduce Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation (VISTA), which preserves the standard OPSD student update while using outcome-verified rollouts to adapt the teacher toward the student distribution. Within each verified rollout, VISTA further restricts this adaptation to the top-$k$ positions with the largest teacher--student KL divergence. Notably, VISTA reuses the rollout and loss function from standard OPSD, introducing no additional sampling or separate reward objective. Across AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25 with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, VISTA achieves the highest Avg@12 at every scale, improving over OPSD by $0.6$, $0.7$, and $2.1$ points, respectively. These results demonstrate the value of student supervision from outcome-verified rollouts and highlight student-to-teacher adaptation as a promising direction for OPSD.

</details>


### [66] [Deriving Scaling Laws for OpenEuroLLM Models: Learning Rate, Batch Size and Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28308)
*Niccolò Ajroldi,Diana Alexandra Onutu,Haider Al-Tahan,Jörg Franke,Sampo Pyysalo,Jenia Jitsev,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了稠密大语言模型在预训练中学习率和批量大小的缩放行为，不仅考察二者的联合最优，还探索它们随模型容量和数据规模的边际演化规律，并建立了相应模型。研究采用Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度，深入分析了学习率退火在不同超参数、模型和数据预算下的收益，以及最优学习率和批量大小在稳定阶段与退火阶段之间的迁移性。此外，还评估了建模损失与模型容量和数据集规模关系的缩放形式。该研究为未来OpenEuroLLM模型的开发建立了首个基线和缩放流程，并开源了全部预训练运行结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型预训练中，学习率与批量大小的缩放规律缺乏系统性的边际演化研究，尤其是在Warmup-Stable-Decay调度下，退火阶段的收益及其与稳定阶段的超参数迁移关系不明确，需要建立统一的缩放模型来指导未来模型开发。

Method: 采用Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度，在英语为主的语料上对稠密LLM进行大规模预训练实验。从两个维度展开：(1)联合最优与边际演化——研究学习率和批量大小随模型容量与数据规模的变化并建立缩放模型；(2)退火分析——考察学习率退火在广泛超参数范围下的收益，以及最优超参数在稳定与退火阶段间的迁移性。同时评估多种建模损失与模型容量、数据集规模关系的缩放形式，特别关注能显式建模二者交互的方法。

Result: 研究发现：能显式建模模型容量与数据集规模交互的缩放形式在捕捉欠训练和过训练两种状态方面尤为有效；学习率退火在广泛设置下能带来收益；最优学习率和批量大小在稳定与退火阶段之间存在一定的迁移性。该项研究为OpenEuroLLM模型建立了首个缩放流程基线，并已开源全部预训练运行。

Conclusion: 该研究为稠密LLM预训练中的学习率与批量大小缩放建立了系统性的基线，验证了能建模容量与数据交互的缩放形式的有效性，为未来OpenEuroLLM模型的开发和训练策略选择提供了可复现的参考框架。

Abstract: We study the scaling behavior of learning rate and batch size in pretraining dense large language models on English-prevalent corpora. Beyond scaling \textit{jointly optimal} learning rates and batch sizes, we investigate their \textit{marginal} evolution with model capacity and data scale and develop a model that captures these relationships. As we employ a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning rate schedule, we further investigate the gains from learning rate annealing over a broad range of hyperparameters settings, models and data budgets, and whether the optimal learning rate and batch size \textit{transfer} between the stable and decay phases.
